path='pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'
src=open(path).read()

old = '''        self._reader_thread = context.Thread(target=self._read_loop, name='mux-reader')
        self._reader_thread.daemon = True
        self._reader_thread.start()
'''
new = '''        self._read_buffer = b''

        self._reader_thread = context.Thread(target=self._read_loop, name='mux-reader')
        self._reader_thread.daemon = True
        self._reader_thread.start()
'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)

old = '''    def _read_frame(self):
        forever = self._underlying.maximum
        header = self._underlying.recvn(_HEADER.size, timeout=forever)
        if len(header) < _HEADER.size:
            raise EOFError('underlying tube closed')
        length, ftype, channel_id = _HEADER.unpack(header)
        payload_len = length - 2
        if payload_len < 0:
            raise EOFError('invalid frame')
        payload = b''
        if payload_len:
            payload = self._underlying.recvn(payload_len, timeout=forever)
            if len(payload) < payload_len:
                raise EOFError('underlying tube closed')
        return ftype, channel_id, payload
'''
new = '''    def _read_frame(self):
        """
        Reads and returns one complete ``(ftype, channel_id, payload)`` frame
        from the underlying tube.

        Uses short reads so that local closure can be observed promptly even
        when the underlying tube does not wake a blocked reader.
        """
        while not self._closed:
            frame = self._extract_frame()
            if frame is not None:
                return frame

            try:
                data = self._underlying.recv(4096, timeout=0.05)
            except EOFError:
                raise

            if data:
                self._read_buffer += data
            elif self._closed:
                break

        raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')

    def _extract_frame(self):
        buf = self._read_buffer
        if len(buf) < _HEADER.size:
            return None
        length, ftype, channel_id = _HEADER.unpack(buf[:_HEADER.size])
        if length < 2:
            raise EOFError('invalid frame')
        total = _HEADER.size + (length - 2)
        if len(buf) < total:
            return None
        payload = buf[_HEADER.size:total]
        self._read_buffer = buf[total:]
        return ftype, channel_id, payload
'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)
open(path,'w').write(src)
print('patched')

patched

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_flow.py <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading
context.log_level = 'error'

def make_pair():
    l = listen(0)
    r = remote('localhost', l.lport)
    l.wait_for_connection()
    return l, r

l,r = make_pair()
m1 = TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
m2 = TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
c1 = m1.open_channel()
c2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c1.settimeout(0.3)

# Send until flow control kicks in
sent = 0
try:
    for i in range(100000):
        c1.send(b'x'*100)
        sent += 100
except TimeoutError:
    print('TimeoutError after sending', sent, 'bytes -> flow control OK')
except Exception as e:
    print('FAIL wrong exception', type(e).__name__, e)
else:
    print('FAIL: never blocked')

# Drain receiver; should trigger resume
time.sleep(0.1)
received = 0
try:
    while True:
        d = c2.recv(timeout=0.1)
        if not d:
            break
        received += len(d)
except EOFError:
    pass
print('received', received, 'bytes')

# Now sender should be unblocked
try:
    c1.send(b'resume-test')
    print('send after drain OK')
except TimeoutError:
    print('FAIL still paused')
except Exception as e:
    print('FAIL', type(e).__name__, e)

# Verify remote received it
print('peer got:', c2.recv())
m1.close(); m2.close()

# ---- flow control independent per channel ----
print("== independent flow ==")
l,r = make_pair()
m1 = TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
m2 = TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
c1 = m1.open_channel(); p1 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c2 = m1.open_channel(); p2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c1.settimeout(0.3)
c2.settimeout(0.3)

# Fill channel 1's buffer to trigger pause
try:
    for i in range(100000):
        c1.send(b'x'*100)
except TimeoutError:
    print('c1 paused OK')
except Exception as e:
    print('FAIL c1', e)

# Channel 2 should still be able to send
p2.send(b'hello on c2')
print('c2 send while c1 paused OK')
print('c2 peer recv:', c2.recv(timeout=1))

# Channel 1 still blocked
try:
    c1.send(b'y'*100)
    print('FAIL c1 should still be paused')
except TimeoutError:
    print('c1 still paused OK')

# Drain channel 1 -> resumes
d = p1.recv(4096)
c1.send(b'after resume')
print('c1 resumed OK:', p1.recv())
m1.close(); m2.close()
print('FLOW TESTS DONE')

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading, random
context.log_level = 'error'

def make_pair():
    l = listen(0)
    r = remote('localhost', l.lport)
    l.wait_for_connection()
    return l, r

l,r = make_pair()
m1 = TubeMultiplexer(l)
m2 = TubeMultiplexer(r)
N = 8
channels1 = []
channels2 = []
for i in range(N):
    c = m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1)
    p = m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
    assert c.channel_id == p.channel_id
    channels1.append(c)
    channels2.append(p)

errors = []
def sender(idx):
    try:
        ch = channels1[idx]
        for j in range(50):
            msg = b'%d:%d:' % (idx, j) + bytes([65 + idx]) * (j % 100)
            ch.send(msg)
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('sender', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

def receiver(idx):
    try:
        ch = channels2[idx]
        for j in range(50):
            expected = b'%d:%d:' % (idx, j) + bytes([65 + idx]) * (j % 100)
            got = ch.recvn(len(expected), timeout=5)
            if got != expected:
                errors.append(('recv', idx, 'mismatch', got, expected))
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('receiver', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

threads = []
for i in range(N):
    t1 = threading.Thread(target=sender, args=(i,)); t1.start(); threads.append(t1)
    t2 = threading.Thread(target=receiver, args=(i,)); t2.start(); threads.append(t2)
for t in threads:
    t.join(10)

print('errors:', errors[:5] if errors else 'NONE')
print('all threads done')
for i in range(N):
    print(i, channels1[i].stats, channels2[i].stats)
m1.close(); m2.close()
print('CONCURRENCY DONE')

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time
context.log_level = 'error'

# mux() method
l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = l.mux(max_channels=10, high_water_mark=100, low_water_mark=10)
m2 = r.mux(max_channels=10, high_water_mark=100, low_water_mark=10)
assert isinstance(m1, TubeMultiplexer)
assert m1.max_channels == 10
print('mux() method OK')
c1 = m1.open_channel(); c2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
assert isinstance(c1, MuxChannel)
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
assert isinstance(c1, tube)
print('MuxChannel subclass of tube OK')
m1.close(); m2.close()

# open_channel timeout with silent peer
p = process(['/bin/sleep', '3'])
try:
    m = TubeMultiplexer(p)
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        m.open_channel(timeout=0.3)
        print('FAIL: no timeout')
    except TimeoutError:
        print('open_channel timeout OK in %.2fs' % (time.time()-t0))
    m.close()
except Exception as e:
    print('process test error', type(e).__name__, e)
try:
    p.close()
except Exception:
    pass

# open_channel on closed mux
l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = TubeMultiplexer(l); m2 = TubeMultiplexer(r)
m1.close()
try:
    m1.open_channel()
    print('FAIL open after close')
except EOFError:
    print('open after close EOFError OK')
m2.close()

# stats frames_sent increments once per send call
l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = TubeMultiplexer(l); m2 = TubeMultiplexer(r)
c1 = m1.open_channel(); c2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c1.send(b'one')
c1.send(b'two'*100)
print('frames_sent:', c1.stats['frames_sent'], 'bytes:', c1.stats['bytes_sent'])
assert c1.stats['frames_sent'] == 2
d1 = c2.recv(); d2 = c2.recv()
print('frames_received:', c2.stats['frames_received'], 'bytes:', c2.stats['bytes_received'])
assert c2.stats['frames_received'] == 2
assert d1 == b'one' and d2 == b'two'*100
print('stats OK')
m1.close(); m2.close()
print('MISC DONE')

"""
Tube multiplexer
================

Implements a multiplexer which allows multiple independent, bidirectional
logical channels over a single underlying tube.
"""

import atexit
import collections
import queue
import struct
import threading
import time

from pwnlib.context import context
from pwnlib.log import getLogger
from pwnlib.tubes.buffer import Buffer
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube

log = getLogger(__name__)

# Frame types
_OPEN   = 1
_ACK    = 2
_NAK    = 3
_DATA   = 4
_CLOSE  = 5
_EOF    = 6   # half-close: sender's outgoing direction is closed
_PAUSE  = 7
_RESUME = 8

_HEADER = struct.Struct('>IBH')  # length, type, channel_id


def _is_channel_id(value):
    """Return True if value is an integer channel id (and not a bool)."""
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)


class TubeMultiplexer(object):
    """
    Multiplexes multiple logical channels over a single underlying tube.

    A :class:`TubeMultiplexer` is created around an existing :class:`~pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube`
    instance.  The remote end of the underlying tube is expected to be another
    :class:`TubeMultiplexer` (or a compatible implementation) speaking the same
    simple frame protocol.

    Channels are created with :meth:`open_channel` and received from the remote
    end with :meth:`accept_channel`.

    Arguments:
        underlying(tube): The tube to multiplex over.
        max_channels(int): Maximum number of simultaneously open channels.
            Must be in the range ``[1, 65535]``.
        high_water_mark(int): Number of bytes buffered per channel at which
            the remote sender is paused.
        low_water_mark(int): Number of bytes buffered per channel at which
            the remote sender is resumed.
    """

    def __init__(self, underlying, max_channels=256,
                 high_water_mark=1048576, low_water_mark=262144):
        if not isinstance(underlying, tube):
            raise TypeError('underlying must be a tube, got %r' % type(underlying).__name__)

        if not isinstance(max_channels, int) or isinstance(max_channels, bool) or \
           not 1 <= max_channels <= 65535:
            raise ValueError('max_channels must be an integer in [1, 65535]')

        if low_water_mark > high_water_mark:
            raise ValueError('low_water_mark cannot be greater than high_water_mark')

        self._underlying = underlying
        self._max_channels = max_channels
        self._high_water_mark = high_water_mark
        self._low_water_mark = low_water_mark

        # channel_id -> MuxChannel currently known locally
        self.channels = {}

        self._closed = False
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._send_lock = threading.Lock()

        # Channels opened by the remote end and waiting for accept_channel()
        self._pending_accept = collections.deque()
        self._accept_cond = threading.Condition(self._lock)

        # channel_id -> queue.Queue used to wait for OPEN acknowledgements
        self._open_results = {}

        # IDs which have been closed locally but are awaiting confirmation
        # from the remote end before they may be reused.
        self._closing_ids = set()

        self._read_buffer = b''

        self._reader_thread = context.Thread(target=self._read_loop, name='mux-reader')
        self._reader_thread.daemon = True
        self._reader_thread.start()

        atexit.register(self.close)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Properties
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def max_channels(self):
        return self._max_channels

    @property
    def high_water_mark(self):
        return self._high_water_mark

    @high_water_mark.setter
    def high_water_mark(self, value):
        if self._low_water_mark is not None and value is not None and self._low_water_mark > value:
            raise ValueError('low_water_mark cannot be greater than high_water_mark')
        self._high_water_mark = value
        for ch in list(self.channels.values()):
            ch._recv_buffer.set_watermarks(high=value)

    @property
    def low_water_mark(self):
        return self._low_water_mark

    @low_water_mark.setter
    def low_water_mark(self, value):
        if value is not None and self._high_water_mark is not None and value > self._high_water_mark:
            raise ValueError('low_water_mark cannot be greater than high_water_mark')
        self._low_water_mark = value
        for ch in list(self.channels.values()):
            ch._recv_buffer.set_watermarks(low=value)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def open_channel(self, channel_id=None, timeout=None):
        """
        Opens a new channel and waits for the remote end to acknowledge it.

        Arguments:
            channel_id(int): The channe
[OUTPUT_TAIL]
           'frames_received': 0,
        }

        self._cond = threading.Condition()

        super(MuxChannel, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def channel_id(self):
        return self._channel_id

    @property
    def stats(self):
        return self._stats

    def close(self):
        """Closes the channel, signalling EOF to the remote peer."""
        with self._cond:
            if self._closed:
                return
            self._closed = True
            self._send_eof = True
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._cond.notify_all()

        self._mux._channel_closed(self)

    def shutdown_raw(self, direction):
        with self._cond:
            if direction == 'send':
                if not self._send_eof:
                    self._send_eof = True
                    send_eof = True
                else:
                    send_eof = False
                self._cond.notify_all()
            elif direction == 'recv':
                self._recv_eof = True
                self._cond.notify_all()
                send_eof = False
            else:
                raise ValueError('direction must be "send" or "recv"')

        if send_eof:
            try:
                self._mux._write_frame(_EOF, self._channel_id)
            except EOFError:
                pass

    def connected_raw(self, direction):
        with self._cond:
            if direction == 'send':
                return not self._closed and not self._send_eof
            if direction == 'recv':
                return not self._closed and not self._recv_eof
            if direction == 'any':
                return not self._closed and (not self._send_eof or not self._recv_eof)
        raise ValueError('direction must be "send", "recv" or "any"')

    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout=0):
        deadline = None
        if timeout is not None:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        with self._cond:
            while not self._recv_buffer and not self._recv_eof and not self._closed:
                if deadline is None:
                    self._cond.wait()
                    continue
                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if remaining <= 0:
                    return False
                self._cond.wait(remaining)
            return bool(self._recv_buffer) or self._recv_eof

    def settimeout_raw(self, timeout):
        pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Raw tube interface
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def recv_raw(self, numb, *a):
        deadline = None
        with self._cond:
            while not self._recv_buffer and not self._recv_eof and not self._closed:
                timeout = self.timeout
                if timeout == 0:
                    return None
                if timeout >= self.maximum:
                    self._cond.wait()
                else:
                    if deadline is None:
                        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
                    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if remaining <= 0:
                        return None
                    self._cond.wait(remaining)

            if not self._recv_buffer:
                raise EOFError('channel closed')

            data = self._recv_buffer.get(numb)

            should_resume = False
            if self._flow_paused_notified and self._recv_buffer.under_low_water:
                self._flow_paused_notified = False
                should_resume = True

        if should_resume:
            try:
                self._mux._write_frame(_RESUME, self._channel_id)
            except EOFError:
                pass

        return data

    def send_raw(self, data):
        deadline = None
        with self._cond:
            while self._flow_paused:
                if self._closed or self._send_eof:
                    raise EOFError('channel closed')
                timeout = self.timeout
                if timeout == 0:
                    raise TimeoutError('timed out waiting for flow control')
                if timeout >= self.maximum:
                    self._cond.wait()
                else:
                    if deadline is None:
                        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
                    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if remaining <= 0:
                        raise TimeoutError('timed out waiting for flow control')
                    self._cond.wait(remaining)

            if self._closed or self._send_eof:
                raise EOFError('channel closed')

        self._mux._write_frame(_DATA, self._channel_id, data)
        self._stats['bytes_sent'] += len(data)
        self._stats['frames_sent'] += 1

   140	    def open_channel(self, channel_id=None, timeout=None):
   141	        """
   142	        Opens a new channel and waits for the remote end to acknowledge it.
   143	
   144	        Arguments:
   145	            channel_id(int): The channel id to use.  When ``None``, a unique
   146	                id in ``[1, max_channels]`` is automatically allocated.
   147	            timeout(float): Maximum time in seconds to wait for the remote
   148	                acknowledgement.
   149	
   150	        Returns:
   151	            MuxChannel: The newly opened channel.
   152	
   153	        Raises:
   154	            TypeError: If ``channel_id`` is not an integer.
   155	            ValueError: If ``channel_id`` is out of range, a duplicate, or
   156	                exceeds the multiplexer capacity.
   157	            TimeoutError: If the remote end does not acknowledge in time.
   158	            EOFError: If the multiplexer is closed.
   159	        """
   160	        with self._lock:
   161	            if self._closed:
   162	                raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   163	
   164	            auto = False
   165	            if channel_id is None:
   166	                channel_id = self._allocate_id()
   167	                auto = True
   168	            else:
   169	                self._validate_channel_id(channel_id)
   170	
   171	            if channel_id in self.channels or channel_id in self._closing_ids:
   172	                raise ValueError('channel id %d is already in use' % channel_id)
   173	            if len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
   174	                raise ValueError('multiplexer has reached its maximum of %d channels' % self._max_channels)
   175	
   176	            ch = MuxChannel(self, channel_id)
   177	            self.channels[channel_id] = ch
   178	            ack_queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=1)
   179	            self._open_results[channel_id] = ack_queue
   180	
   181	        try:
   182	            self._write_frame(_OPEN, channel_id)
   183	        except EOFError:
   184	            self._discard_open(channel_id, ch)
   185	            raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   186	
   187	        try:
   188	            result = ack_queue.get(timeout=timeout)
   189	        except queue.Empty:
   190	            self._discard_open(channel_id, ch)
   191	            raise TimeoutError('timed out waiting for acknowledgement of channel %d' % channel_id)
   192	
   193	        if result is False:
   194	            self._discard_open(channel_id, ch)
   195	            if auto:
   196	                # The remote rejected our auto-allocated id (likely a
   197	                # simultaneous allocation).  Retry with a fresh id.
   198	                return self.open_channel(None, timeout=timeout)
   199	            raise ValueError('remote end rejected channel id %d' % channel_id)
   200	
   201	        if result == 'closed':
   202	            self._discard_open(channel_id, ch)
   203	            raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   204	
   205	        return ch
   206	
   207	    def accept_channel(self, timeout=None):
   208	        """
   209	        Waits for the remote end to open a channel.
   210	
   211	        Arguments:
   212	            timeout(float): Maximum time in seconds to wait.
   213	
   214	        Returns:
   215	            MuxChannel: The accepted channel, or ``None`` on timeout.
   216	
   217	        Raises:
   218	            EOFError: If the multiplexer is closed.
   219	        """
   220	        deadline = None
   221	        if timeout is not None:
   222	            deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
   223	
   224	        with self._accept_cond:
   225	            while not self._pending_accept and not self._closed:
   226	                if deadline is None:
   227	                    self._accept_cond.wait()
   228	                else:
   229	                    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
   230	                    if remaining <= 0:
   231	                        return None
   232	                    self._accept_cond.wait(remaining)
   233	
   234	            if self._closed:
   235	                raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   236	
   237	            if not self._pending_accept:
   238	                return None
   239	
   240	            return self._pending_accept.popleft()
   241	
   242	    def close(self):
   243	        """Closes all channels and the underlying tube.  Idempotent."""
   244	        with self._lock:
   245	            if self._closed:
   246	                return
   247	            self._closed = True
   248	
   249	        # Unblock any thread waiting in accept_channel()
   250	        with self._accept_cond:
   251	            self._accept_cond.notify_all()
   252	
   253	        # Signal EOF to every local channel
   254	        for ch in list(self.channels.values()):
   255	            with ch._cond:
   256	                ch._closed = True
   257	                ch._send_eof = True
   258	                ch._recv_eof = True
   259	                ch._cond.notify_all()
   260	
   261	        # Wake any threads waiting for an OPEN acknowledgement
   262	        with self._lock:
   263	            for ack_queue in self._open_results.values():
   264	                try:
   265	                    ack_queue.put_nowait('closed')
   266	                except queue.Full:
   267	                    pass
   268	            self._open_results.clear()
   269	
   270	        try:
   271	            self._underlying.close()
   272	        except Exception:
   273	            pass
   274	
   275	        # Give the reader thread a moment to notice the EOF, but do not block
   276	        # forever if it is stuck.
   277	        if self._reader_thread.is_alive() and self._reader_thread is not threading.current_thread():
   278	            self._reader_thread.join(timeout=1.0)
   279	
   280	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   281	    # Channel registration helpers
   282	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   283	    def _validate_channel_id(self, channel_id):
   284	        if not _is_channel_id(channel_id):
   285	            raise TypeError('channel id must be an integer, got %r' % type(channel_id).__name__)
   286	        if not 1 <= channel_id <= 65535:
   287	            raise ValueError('channel id must be in [1, 65535]')
   288	        if channel_id > self._max_channels:
   289	            raise ValueError('channel id %d exceeds max_channels %d' % (channel_id, self._max_channels))
   290	
   291	    def _allocate_id(self):
   292	        for cid in range(1, self._max_channels + 1):
   293	            if cid not in self.channels and cid not in self._closing_ids:
   294	                return cid
   295	        raise ValueError('multiplexer has reached its maximum of %d channels' % self._max_channels)
   296	
   297	    def _discard_open(self, channel_id, ch):
   298	        with self._lock:
   299	            self._open_results.pop(channel_id, None)
   300	            if self.channels.get(channel_id) is ch:
   301	                del self.channels[channel_id]
   302	        try:
   303	            self._write_frame(_CLOSE, channel_id)
   304	        except EOFError:
   305	            pass
   306	
   307	    def _channel_closed(self, ch):
   308	        """Called when the local user closes a channel."""
   309	        with self._lock:
   310	            if self.channels.get(ch.channel_id) is ch:
   311	                del self.channels[ch.channel_id]
   312	            self._closing_ids.add(ch.channel_id)
   313	        try:
   314	            self._write_frame(_CLOSE, ch.channel_id)
   315	        except EOFError:
   316	            pass
   317	
   318	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   319	    # Frame protocol
   320	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   321	    def _write_frame(self, ftype, channel_id, payload=b''):
   322	        with self._lock:
   323	            if self._closed:
   324	                raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   325	        data = _HEADER.pack(2 + len(payload), ftype, channel_id) + payload
   326	        with self._send_lock:
   327	            if self._closed:
   328	                raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   329	            try:
   330	                self._underlying.send(data)

END STEP

returncode=0
   330	                self._underlying.send(data)
   331	            except EOFError:
   332	                self.close()
   333	                raise
   334	
   335	    def _read_frame(self):
   336	        """
   337	        Reads and returns one complete ``(ftype, channel_id, payload)`` frame
   338	        from the underlying tube.
   339	
   340	        Uses short reads so that local closure can be observed promptly even
   341	        when the underlying tube does not wake a blocked reader.
   342	        """
   343	        while not self._closed:
   344	            frame = self._extract_frame()
   345	            if frame is not None:
   346	                return frame
   347	
   348	            try:
   349	                data = self._underlying.recv(4096, timeout=0.05)
   350	            except EOFError:
   351	                raise
   352	
   353	            if data:
   354	                self._read_buffer += data
   355	            elif self._closed:
   356	                break
   357	
   358	        raise EOFError('multiplexer is closed')
   359	
   360	    def _extract_frame(self):
   361	        buf = self._read_buffer
   362	        if len(buf) < _HEADER.size:
   363	            return None
   364	        length, ftype, channel_id = _HEADER.unpack(buf[:_HEADER.size])
   365	        if length < 2:
   366	            raise EOFError('invalid frame')
   367	        total = _HEADER.size + (length - 2)
   368	        if len(buf) < total:
   369	            return None
   370	        payload = buf[_HEADER.size:total]
   371	        self._read_buffer = buf[total:]
   372	        return ftype, channel_id, payload
   373	
   374	    def _read_loop(self):
   375	        try:
   376	            while True:
   377	                ftype, channel_id, payload = self._read_frame()
   378	                self._handle_frame(ftype, channel_id, payload)
   379	        except EOFError:
   380	            pass
   381	        except Exception as e:
   382	            log.debug('multiplexer reader error: %r', e)
   383	        finally:
   384	            # The underlying tube has died; propagate EOF to all channels.
   385	            self.close()
   386	
   387	    def _handle_frame(self, ftype, channel_id, payload):
   388	        if ftype == _OPEN:
   389	            self._handle_open(channel_id)
   390	        elif ftype == _ACK:
   391	            self._handle_ack(channel_id)
   392	        elif ftype == _NAK:
   393	            self._handle_nak(channel_id)
   394	        elif ftype == _DATA:
   395	            self._handle_data(channel_id, payload)
   396	        elif ftype == _CLOSE:
   397	            self._handle_close(channel_id)
   398	        elif ftype == _EOF:
   399	            self._handle_eof(channel_id)
   400	        elif ftype == _PAUSE:
   401	            self._handle_pause(channel_id)
   402	        elif ftype == _RESUME:
   403	            self._handle_resume(channel_id)
   404	
   405	    def _handle_open(self, channel_id):
   406	        reject = False
   407	        with self._lock:
   408	            if self._closed:
   409	                return
   410	            if channel_id in self.channels or channel_id in self._closing_ids:
   411	                reject = True
   412	            elif len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
   413	                reject = True
   414	            else:
   415	                ch = MuxChannel(self, channel_id)
   416	                self.channels[channel_id] = ch
   417	                self._pending_accept.append(ch)
   418	                with self._accept_cond:
   419	                    self._accept_cond.notify_all()
   420	
   421	        if reject:
   422	            try:
   423	                self._write_frame(_NAK, channel_id)
   424	            except EOFError:
   425	                pass
   426	            return
   427	
   428	        try:
   429	            self._write_frame(_ACK, channel_id)
   430	        except EOFError:
   431	            pass
   432	
   433	    def _handle_ack(self, channel_id):
   434	        with self._lock:
   435	            ack_queue = self._open_results.pop(channel_id, None)
   436	            if ack_queue is not None:
   437	                try:
   438	                    ack_queue.put_nowait(True)
   439	                except queue.Full:
   440	                    pass
   441	
   442	    def _handle_nak(self, channel_id):
   443	        with self._lock:
   444	            ack_queue = self._open_results.pop(channel_id, None)
   445	            if ack_queue is not None:
   446	                try:
   447	                    ack_queue.put_nowait(False)
   448	                except queue.Full:
   449	                    pass
   450	
   451	    def _handle_data(self, channel_id, payload):
   452	        ch = self._get_channel(channel_id)
   453	        if ch is None:
   454	            return
   455	        send_pause = False
   456	        with ch._cond:
   457	            ch._recv_buffer.add(payload)
   458	            ch._stats['bytes_received'] += len(payload)
   459	            ch._stats['frames_received'] += 1
   460	            if ch._recv_buffer.over_high_water and not ch._flow_paused_notified:
   461	                ch._flow_paused_notified = True
   462	                send_pause = True
   463	            ch._cond.notify_all()
   464	        if send_pause:
   465	            try:
   466	                self._write_frame(_PAUSE, channel_id)
   467	            except EOFError:
   468	                pass
   469	
   470	    def _handle_close(self, channel_id):

END STEP

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading
context.log_level = 'error'

def make_pair():
    l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
    return l, r

# Remote accept blocked, local closes -> remote accept EOFError
print("== remote accept blocked on close ==")
l,r = make_pair(); m1=TubeMultiplexer(l); m2=TubeMultiplexer(r)
res=[]
def acc():
    try:
        res.append(m2.accept_channel())
    except EOFError:
        res.append('EOF')
    except Exception as e:
        res.append(type(e).__name__)
t=threading.Thread(target=acc); t.daemon=True; t.start()
time.sleep(0.2)
m1.close()
t.join(3)
print('result', res)
assert res == ['EOF'], res
m2.close()

# Sender blocked by flow control, remote closes -> sender EOFError
print("== sender blocked, remote closes ==")
l,r = make_pair()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
c=m1.open_channel(); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c.settimeout(1.0)
try:
    for i in range(100000):
        c.send(b'x'*100)
except TimeoutError:
    print('c paused')
except Exception as e:
    print('unexpected', e)
res=[]
def snd():
    try:
        c.send(b'blocked')
    except EOFError:
        res.append('EOF')
    except Exception as e:
        res.append(type(e).__name__)
t=threading.Thread(target=snd); t.daemon=True; t.start()
time.sleep(0.2)
m2.close()  # closes remote underlying, should wake sender with EOF
t.join(3)
print('sender result', res)
assert res == ['EOF'], res
m1.close()

# Open blocked waiting for ack, remote closes -> EOFError
print("== open blocked, remote closes ==")
l,r = make_pair()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l); m2=TubeMultiplexer(r)
res=[]
def op():
    try:
        res.append(m1.open_channel(timeout=None))
    except EOFError:
        res.append('EOF')
    except Exception as e:
        res.append(type(e).__name__)
t=threading.Thread(target=op); t.daemon=True; t.start()
# ensure OPEN sent before close
time.sleep(0.3)
m2.close()
t.join(3)
print('open result', res)
assert res == ['EOF'], res
m1.close()

# recvall after half-close
print("== recvall after half-close ==")
l,r = make_pair(); m1=TubeMultiplexer(l); m2=TubeMultiplexer(r)
c=m1.open_channel(); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c.send(b'hello')
c.send(b'world')
c.shutdown('send')
data = p.recvall(timeout=2)
print('recvall:', data)
assert data == b'helloworld'
m1.close(); m2.close()
print('EDGE TESTS DONE')

returncode=0
class Thread(threading.Thread):
    """
    Instantiates a context-aware thread, which inherit its context when it is
    instantiated. The class can be accessed both on the context module as
    `pwnlib.context.Thread` and on the context singleton object inside the
    context module as `pwnlib.context.context.Thread`.

    Threads created by using the native :class`threading`.Thread` will have a
    clean (default) context.

    Regardless of the mechanism used to create any thread, the context
    is de-coupled from the parent thread, so changes do not cascade
    to child or parent.

    Saves a copy of the context when instantiated (at ``__init__``)
    and updates the new thread's context before passing control
    to the user code via ``run`` or ``target=``.

    Examples:

        >>> context.clear()
        >>> context.update(arch='arm')
        >>> def p():
        ...     print(context.arch)
        ...     context.arch = 'mips'
        ...     print(context.arch)
        >>> # Note that a normal Thread starts with a clean context
        >>> # (i386 is the default architecture)
        >>> t = threading.Thread(target=p)
        >>> _=(t.start(), t.join())
        i386
        mips
        >>> # Note that the main Thread's context is unchanged
        >>> print(context.arch)
        arm
        >>> # Note that a context-aware Thread receives a copy of the context
        >>> t = pwnlib.context.Thread(target=p)
        >>> _=(t.start(), t.join())
        arm
        mips
        >>> # Again, the main thread is unchanged
        >>> print(context.arch)
        arm

    Implementation Details:

        This class implemented by hooking the private function
        :func:`threading.Thread._Thread_bootstrap`, which is called before
        passing control to :func:`threading.Thread.run`.

        This could be done by overriding ``run`` itself, but we would have to
        ensure that all uses of the class would only ever use the keyword
        ``target=`` for ``__init__``, or that all subclasses invoke
        ``super(Subclass.self).set_up_context()`` or similar.
    """
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(Thread, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.old = context.copy()

    def __bootstrap(self):
        """
        Implementation Details:
            This only works because the class is named ``Thread``.
            If its name is changed, we have to implement this hook
            differently.
        """
        context.update(**self.old)
        sup = super(Thread, self)
        bootstrap = getattr(sup, '_bootstrap', None)
        if bootstrap is None:
            sup.__bootstrap()
        else:
            bootstrap()
    _bootstrap = __bootstrap

def _longest(d):
    """
    Returns an OrderedDict with the contents of the input dictionary ``d``
    sorted by the length of the keys, in descending order.

    This is useful for performing substring matching via ``str.startswith``,
    as it ensures the most complete match will be found.


END STEP

STEP 73 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_stress.py <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading, random
context.log_level = 'error'

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)

N=20
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1)
    p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(2)
    p.settimeout(2)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

errors=[]
def sender(idx):
    try:
        ch=c1s[idx]
        random.seed(idx)
        for j in range(100):
            payload = bytes([idx]) * (random.randint(1,500))
            ch.send(payload)
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('s', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

def receiver(idx):
    try:
        ch=c2s[idx]
        random.seed(idx)
        total = 0
        for j in range(100):
            expected = bytes([idx]) * (random.randint(1,500))
            # use recvn to get exact payload
            got = ch.recvn(len(expected), timeout=5)
            if got != expected:
                errors.append(('r', idx, 'mismatch', len(got), len(expected)))
                return
            total += len(got)
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('r', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('errors:', errors[:10] if errors else 'NONE')
print('threads alive:', sum(t.is_alive() for t in threads))

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_stress2.py <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading, random
context.log_level = 'error'

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)

N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1)
    p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(30)
    p.settimeout(30)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

errors=[]
def sender(idx):
    try:
        ch=c1s[idx]
        random.seed(idx)
        for j in range(100):
            payload = bytes([idx]) * (random.randint(1,500))
            ch.send(payload)
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('s', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

def receiver(idx):
    try:
        ch=c2s[idx]
        random.seed(idx)
        for j in range(100):
            expected = bytes([idx]) * (random.randint(1,500))
            got = ch.recvn(len(expected), timeout=15)
            if got != expected:
                errors.append(('r', idx, 'mismatch', j, len(got), len(expected)))
                return
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('r', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(40)
print('errors:', errors[:10] if errors else 'NONE')
print('threads alive:', sum(t.is_alive() for t in threads))
for i in range(N):
    print('stats', i, c1s[i].stats['frames_sent'], c1s[i].stats['bytes_sent'], c2s[i].stats['frames_received'], c2s[i].stats['bytes_received'])
m1.close(); m2.close()
print('DONE')

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(30); p.settimeout(30)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

slow=[]
def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        t0=time.time()
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=15)
        dt=time.time()-t0
        if dt>1:
            slow.append((idx,j,dt,len(got),len(expected)))
        if got != expected:
            print('MISMATCH', idx, j, len(got), len(expected), 'dt', dt)
            return

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
t0=time.time()
for t in threads:
    t.join(40)
print('total time', time.time()-t0)
print('slow reads (>1s):', slow[:20], 'count', len(slow))
for i in range(N):
    print('stats', i, c1s[i].stats['frames_sent'], c2s[i].stats['frames_received'])
m1.close(); m2.close()

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_debug2.py <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

log_lock = threading.Lock()
def log(msg):
    with log_lock:
        with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
            f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time(), msg))

orig_send_raw = MuxChannel.send_raw
def send_raw_log(self, data):
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        return orig_send_raw(self, data)
    finally:
        dt=time.time()-t0
        if dt>0.3:
            log('send_raw ch%d took %.3f' % (self.channel_id, dt))
MuxChannel.send_raw = send_raw_log

orig_handle_data = TubeMultiplexer._handle_data
def handle_data_log(self, channel_id, payload):
    t0=time.time()
    orig_handle_data(self, channel_id, payload)
    dt=time.time()-t0
    if dt>0.3:
        log('handle_data ch%d took %.3f' % (channel_id, dt))
TubeMultiplexer._handle_data = handle_data_log

orig_recv_raw = MuxChannel.recv_raw
def recv_raw_log(self, numb, *a):
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        return orig_recv_raw(self, numb, *a)
    finally:
        dt=time.time()-t0
        if dt>0.3:
            log('recv_raw ch%d took %.3f' % (self.channel_id, dt))
MuxChannel.recv_raw = recv_raw_log

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=20)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d %d %d' % (idx, j, len(got), len(expected)))
            return

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(40)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random, sys, traceback
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            print('MISMATCH', idx, j, len(got), len(expected))
            print('m2 closed?', m2._closed)
            print('--- m2 reader stack ---')
            traceback.print_stack(sys._current_frames()[m2._reader_thread.ident])
            print('--- m1 reader stack ---')
            traceback.print_stack(sys._current_frames()[m1._reader_thread.ident])
            return

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random, sys, traceback
context.log_level='error'

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

start = time.time()
def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time()-start, msg))

orig_handle_data = TubeMultiplexer._handle_data
def handle_data_log(self, channel_id, payload):
    log('READY data ch%d len%d' % (channel_id, len(payload)))
    orig_handle_data(self, channel_id, payload)
    log('DONE data ch%d' % channel_id)
TubeMultiplexer._handle_data = handle_data_log

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d' % (idx, j))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_debug6.py <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

start=time.time()
def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time()-start, msg))

orig_recv_raw = MuxChannel.recv_raw
def recv_raw_log(self, numb, *a):
    with self._cond:
        buflen = len(self._recv_buffer)
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        res = orig_recv_raw(self, numb, *a)
    except Exception as e:
        log('recv_raw ch%d -> EXC %s (buf was %d, waited %.3f)' % (self.channel_id, type(e).__name__, buflen, time.time()-t0))
        raise
    dt=time.time()-t0
    if self.channel_id == 2:
        log('recv_raw ch%d (numb=%d buf=%d) -> %d in %.4f' % (self.channel_id, numb, buflen, 0 if res is None else len(res), dt))
    return res
MuxChannel.recv_raw = recv_raw_log

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d len got=%d expected=%d' % (idx, j, len(got), len(expected)))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

start=time.time()
def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time()-start, msg))

orig_recv_raw = MuxChannel.recv_raw
def recv_raw_log(self, numb, *a):
    with self._cond:
        buflen = len(self._recv_buffer)
        stop = self._stop
        to = self._timeout
    if self.channel_id == 2:
        log('ENTER recv_raw ch2 buf=%d stop=%s timeout=%s' % (buflen, stop, to))
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        res = orig_recv_raw(self, numb, *a)
    except Exception as e:
        if self.channel_id == 2:
            log('EXIT recv_raw ch2 EXC %s after %.3f' % (type(e).__name__, time.time()-t0))
        raise
    dt=time.time()-t0
    if self.channel_id == 2:
        log('EXIT recv_raw ch2 -> %s after %.3f' % ('None' if res is None else len(res), dt))
    return res
MuxChannel.recv_raw = recv_raw_log

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        t0=time.time()
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        dt=time.time()-t0
        if idx==2:
            log('recvn ch2 frame%d expected%d got%d dt%.3f' % (j, len(expected), len(got), dt))
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d' % (idx, j))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

start=time.time()
def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time()-start, msg))

orig_recvn = MuxChannel.recvn
def recvn_log(self, numb, timeout=None):
    with self._cond:
        blen = len(self.buffer)
        rlen = len(self._recv_buffer)
    if self.channel_id == 2:
        log('recvn ch2 ENTER n=%d buf=%d rbuf=%d timeout=%s' % (numb, blen, rlen, timeout))
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        res = orig_recvn(self, numb, timeout)
    except Exception as e:
        if self.channel_id==2:
            log('recvn ch2 EXC %s after %.3f' % (type(e).__name__, time.time()-t0))
        raise
    if self.channel_id==2:
        log('recvn ch2 EXIT n=%d got=%d dt=%.3f' % (numb, len(res), time.time()-t0))
    return res
MuxChannel.recvn = recvn_log

orig_fill = MuxChannel._fillbuffer
def fill_log(self, timeout=None):
    if self.channel_id == 2:
        log('fill ch2 ENTER timeout=%s buf=%d rbuf=%d stop=%s' % (timeout, len(self.buffer), len(self._recv_buffer), self._stop))
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        res = orig_fill(self, timeout)
    except Exception as e:
        if self.channel_id==2:
            log('fill ch2 EXC %s after %.3f' % (type(e).__name__, time.time()-t0))
        raise
    if self.channel_id==2:
        log('fill ch2 EXIT -> %r dt=%.3f' % (None if res is None else len(res), time.time()-t0))
    return res
MuxChannel._fillbuffer = fill_log

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d' % (idx, j))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

start=time.time()
def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time()-start, msg))

orig_send_raw = MuxChannel.send_raw
def send_raw_log(self, data):
    t0=time.time()
    if self.channel_id==2:
        log('send ch2 START len%d paused=%s' % (len(data), self._flow_paused))
    try:
        res = orig_send_raw(self, data)
    except Exception as e:
        if self.channel_id==2:
            log('send ch2 EXC %s after %.3f' % (type(e).__name__, time.time()-t0))
        raise
    if self.channel_id==2:
        log('send ch2 END len%d after %.3f' % (len(data), time.time()-t0))
    return res
MuxChannel.send_raw = send_raw_log

orig_pause = TubeMultiplexer._handle_pause
def pause_log(self, cid):
    if cid==2:
        log('m1 PAUSE ch2')
    return orig_pause(self, cid)
TubeMultiplexer._handle_pause = pause_log

orig_resume = TubeMultiplexer._handle_resume
def resume_log(self, cid):
    if cid==2:
        log('m1 RESUME ch2')
    return orig_resume(self, cid)
TubeMultiplexer._handle_resume = resume_log

orig_write = TubeMultiplexer._write_frame
def write_log(self, ftype, cid, payload=b''):
    if cid==2 and ftype in (muxmod._PAUSE, muxmod._RESUME, muxmod._DATA):
        log('m2 WRITE type%d ch2 len%d' % (ftype, len(payload)))
    return orig_write(self, ftype, cid, payload)
TubeMultiplexer._write_frame = write_log

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d' % (idx, j))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random
context.log_level='error'
import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

start=time.time()
def tid(): return threading.current_thread().name
def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s %s\n' % (time.time()-start, tid(), msg))

orig_recv_raw = MuxChannel.recv_raw
def recv_raw_log(self, numb, *a):
    with self._cond:
        buflen = len(self._recv_buffer)
    if self.channel_id==2:
        log('ENTER recv_raw buf=%d' % buflen)
    t0=time.time()
    res = orig_recv_raw(self, numb, *a)
    if self.channel_id==2:
        log('EXIT recv_raw -> %s after %.3f' % ('None' if res is None else len(res), time.time()-t0))
    return res
MuxChannel.recv_raw = recv_raw_log

def sender(idx):
    threading.current_thread().name = 'sender-%d' % idx
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    threading.current_thread().name = 'receiver-%d' % idx
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d' % (idx, j))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print('done')
m1.close(); m2.close()

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
import time, threading, random

import pwnlib.tubes.mux as muxmod

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=4
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(60); p.settimeout(60)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

def log(msg):
    with open('/tmp/mux_debug.log','a') as f:
        f.write('%.3f %s\n' % (time.time()-start, msg))

orig_recv_raw = MuxChannel.recv_raw
def recv_raw_log(self, numb, *a):
    with self._cond:
        buflen = len(self._recv_buffer)
    which = 'm1' if self._mux is m1 else 'm2'
    log('ENTER recv_raw %s cid%d buf=%d' % (which, self.channel_id, buflen))
    t0=time.time()
    res = orig_recv_raw(self, numb, *a)
    log('EXIT recv_raw %s cid%d -> %s after %.3f' % (which, self.channel_id, 'None' if res is None else len(res), time.time()-t0))
    return res

def sender(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        c1s[idx].send(bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500)))
    log('SENDER %d done' % idx)

def receiver(idx):
    random.seed(idx)
    for j in range(100):
        expected = bytes([idx])*(random.randint(1,500))
        got = c2s[idx].recvn(len(expected), timeout=8)
        if got != expected:
            log('MISMATCH %d %d' % (idx, j))
            return
    log('RECEIVER %d done' % idx)

for attempt in range(3):
    threads=[]
    for i in range(N):
        t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
        t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    for t in threads:
        t.join(30)
    if any(open('/tmp/mux_debug.log').read().count('MISMATCH') for _ in [0]):
        break
print('done attempt')
m1.close(); m2.close()

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_stress_fixed.py <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time, threading, random
context.log_level = 'error'

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
m2=TubeMultiplexer(r, high_water_mark=4096, low_water_mark=1024)
N=8
c1s=[]; c2s=[]
for i in range(N):
    c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=i+1)
    p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=5)
    c.settimeout(30); p.settimeout(30)
    c1s.append(c); c2s.append(p)

errors=[]
def sender(idx):
    try:
        rng = random.Random(idx)
        ch=c1s[idx]
        for j in range(200):
            payload = bytes([idx]) * rng.randint(1,500)
            ch.send(payload)
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('s', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

def receiver(idx):
    try:
        rng = random.Random(idx)
        ch=c2s[idx]
        for j in range(200):
            expected = bytes([idx]) * rng.randint(1,500)
            got = ch.recvn(len(expected), timeout=10)
            if got != expected:
                errors.append(('r', idx, 'mismatch', j, len(got), len(expected)))
                return
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(('r', idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

threads=[]
for i in range(N):
    t=threading.Thread(target=sender,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t=threading.Thread(target=receiver,args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(40)
print('errors:', errors[:10] if errors else 'NONE')
print('threads alive:', sum(t.is_alive() for t in threads))
for i in range(N):
    a=c1s[i].stats; b=c2s[i].stats
    assert a['frames_sent']==200 and b['frames_received']==200, (i,a,b)
m1.close(); m2.close()
print('STRESS FIXED DONE')

from pwn import *

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
     1	"""
     2	Tube multiplexer
     3	================
     4	
     5	Implements a multiplexer which allows multiple independent, bidirectional
     6	logical channels over a single underlying tube.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	import atexit
    10	import collections
    11	import queue
    12	import struct
    13	import threading
    14	import time
    15	
    16	from pwnlib.context import context
    17	from pwnlib.log import getLogger
    18	from pwnlib.tubes.buffer import Buffer
    19	from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
    20	
    21	log = getLogger(__name__)
    22	
    23	# Frame types
    24	_OPEN   = 1
    25	_ACK    = 2
    26	_NAK    = 3
    27	_DATA   = 4
    28	_CLOSE  = 5
    29	_EOF    = 6   # half-close: sender's outgoing direction is closed
    30	_PAUSE  = 7
    31	_RESUME = 8
    32	
    33	_HEADER = struct.Struct('>IBH')  # length, type, channel_id
    34	
    35	
    36	def _is_channel_id(value):
    37	    """Return True if value is an integer channel id (and not a bool)."""
    38	    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
    39	
    40	
    41	class TubeMultiplexer(object):
    42	    """
    43	    Multiplexes multiple logical channels over a single underlying tube.
    44	
    45	    A :class:`TubeMultiplexer` is created around an existing :class:`~pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube`
    46	    instance.  The remote end of the underlying tube is expected to be another
    47	    :class:`TubeMultiplexer` (or a compatible implementation) speaking the same
    48	    simple frame protocol.
    49	
    50	    Channels are created with :meth:`open_channel` and received from the remote
    51	    end with :meth:`accept_channel`.
    52	
    53	    Arguments:
    54	        underlying(tube): The tube to multiplex over.
    55	        max_channels(int): Maximum number of simultaneously open channels.
    56	            Must be in the range ``[1, 65535]``.
    57	        high_water_mark(int): Number of bytes buffered per channel at which
    58	            the remote sender is paused.
    59	        low_water_mark(int): Number of bytes buffered per channel at which
    60	            the remote sender is resumed.
    61	    """
    62	
    63	    def __init__(self, underlying, max_channels=256,
    64	                 high_water_mark=1048576, low_water_mark=262144):
    65	        if not isinstance(underlying, tube):
    66	            raise TypeError('underlying must be a tube, got %r' % type(underlying).__name__)
    67	
    68	        if not isinstance(max_channels, int) or isinstance(max_channels, bool) or \
    69	           not 1 <= max_channels <= 65535:
    70	            raise ValueError('max_channels must be an integer in [1, 65535]')
    71	
    72	        if low_water_mark > high_water_mark:
    73	            raise ValueError('low_water_mark cannot be greater than high_water_mark')
    74	
    75	        self._underlying = underlying
    76	        self._max_channels = max_channels
    77	        self._high_water_mark = high_water_mark
    78	        self._low_water_mark = low_water_mark
    79	
    80	        # channel_id -> MuxChannel currently known locally
    81	        self.channels = {}
    82	
    83	        self._closed = False
    84	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
    85	        self._send_lock = threading.Lock()
    86	
    87	        # Channels opened by the remote end and waiting for accept_channel()
    88	        self._pending_accept = collections.deque()
    89	        self._accept_cond = threading.Condition(self._lock)
    90	
    91	        # channel_id -> queue.Queue used to wait for OPEN acknowledgements
    92	        self._open_results = {}
    93	
    94	        # IDs which have been closed locally but are awaiting confirmation
    95	        # from the remote end before they may be reused.
    96	        self._closing_ids = set()
    97	
    98	        self._read_buffer = b''
    99	
   100	        self._reader_thread = context.Thread(target=self._read_loop, name='mux-reader')
   101	        self._reader_thread.daemon = True
   102	        self._reader_thread.start()
   103	
   104	        atexit.register(self.close)
   105	
   106	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   107	    # Properties
   108	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   109	    @property
   110	    def max_channels(self):
   111	        return self._max_channels
   112	
   113	    @property
   114	    def high_water_mark(self):
   115	        return self._high_water_mark
   116	
   117	    @high_water_mark.setter
   118	    def high_water_mark(self, value):
   119	        if self._low_water_mark is not None and value is not None and self._low_water_mark > value:
   120	            raise ValueError('low_water_mark cannot be greater than high_water_mark')
   121	        self._high_water_mark = value
   122	        for ch in list(self.channels.values()):
   123	            ch._recv_buffer.set_watermarks(high=value, low=self._low_water_mark)
   124	
   125	    @property
   126	    def low_water_mark(self):
   127	        return self._low_water_mark
   128	
   129	    @low_water_mark.setter
   130	    def low_water_mark(self, value):
   131	        if value is not None and self._high_water_mark is not None and value > self._high_water_mark:
   132	            raise ValueError('low_water_mark cannot be greater than high_water_mark')
   133	        self._low_water_mark = value
   134	        for ch in list(self.channels.values()):
   135	            ch._recv_buffer.set_watermarks(high=self._high_water_mark, low=value)
   136	
   137	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   138	    # Public API
   139	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   140	    def open_channel(self, channel_id=None, timeout=None):

END STEP

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
   470	    def _handle_close(self, channel_id):
   471	        with self._lock:
   472	            ch = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   473	            if ch is not None:
   474	                del self.channels[channel_id]
   475	            if channel_id in self._closing_ids:
   476	                self._closing_ids.discard(channel_id)
   477	                confirm = False
   478	            else:
   479	                confirm = True
   480	
   481	        if ch is not None:
   482	            with ch._cond:
   483	                ch._closed = True
   484	                ch._send_eof = True
   485	                ch._recv_eof = True
   486	                ch._cond.notify_all()
   487	
   488	        if confirm and ch is not None:
   489	            try:
   490	                self._write_frame(_CLOSE, channel_id)
   491	            except EOFError:
   492	                pass
   493	
   494	    def _handle_eof(self, channel_id):
   495	        ch = self._get_channel(channel_id)
   496	        if ch is None:
   497	            return
   498	        with ch._cond:
   499	            ch._recv_eof = True
   500	            ch._cond.notify_all()
   501	
   502	    def _handle_pause(self, channel_id):
   503	        ch = self._get_channel(channel_id)
   504	        if ch is None:
   505	            return
   506	        with ch._cond:
   507	            ch._flow_paused = True
   508	
   509	    def _handle_resume(self, channel_id):
   510	        ch = self._get_channel(channel_id)
   511	        if ch is None:
   512	            return
   513	        with ch._cond:
   514	            ch._flow_paused = False
   515	            ch._cond.notify_all()
   516	
   517	    def _get_channel(self, channel_id):
   518	        with self._lock:
   519	            return self.channels.get(channel_id)
   520	
   521	
   522	class MuxChannel(tube):
   523	    """
   524	    A single logical channel within a :class:`TubeMultiplexer`.
   525	
   526	    Behaves like a normal :class:`~pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube`, but all data is
   527	    multiplexed over the underlying tube of the :class:`TubeMultiplexer`.
   528	    """
   529	
   530	    def __init__(self, mux, channel_id, *args, **kwargs):
   531	        self._mux = mux
   532	        self._channel_id = channel_id
   533	
   534	        self._recv_buffer = Buffer()
   535	        self._recv_buffer.set_watermarks(high=mux.high_water_mark, low=mux.low_water_mark)
   536	
   537	        self._send_eof = False
   538	        self._recv_eof = False
   539	        self._closed = False
   540	        self._flow_paused = False
   541	        self._flow_paused_notified = False
   542	
   543	        self._stats = {
   544	            'bytes_sent': 0,
   545	            'bytes_received': 0,
   546	            'frames_sent': 0,
   547	            'frames_received': 0,
   548	        }
   549	
   550	        self._cond = threading.Condition()
   551	
   552	        super(MuxChannel, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)
   553	
   554	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   555	    # Public API
   556	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   557	    @property
   558	    def channel_id(self):
   559	        return self._channel_id
   560	

END STEP

STEP 98 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && timeout 60 python - <<'PY'
from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
from pwnlib.tubes.buffer import Buffer
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
import time, threading
context.log_level='error'

# Buffer API
b=Buffer(); b.set_watermarks(high=100, low=50)
assert b.high_water==100 and b.low_water==50 and not b.over_high_water and not b.under_low_water
b.add(b'A'*100); assert b.over_high_water and not b.under_low_water
b.get(50); assert not b.over_high_water and b.under_low_water
try: b.set_watermarks(high=1, low=2); assert False
except ValueError: pass

# mux method
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=l.mux(max_channels=64, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
m2=r.mux(max_channels=64, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
assert isinstance(m1, TubeMultiplexer) and isinstance(m2, TubeMultiplexer)
assert m1.high_water_mark==1000 and m1.low_water_mark==100

# channels
c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=7); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
assert m1.channels[7] is c and m2.channels[7] is p
assert isinstance(c, tube) and isinstance(c, MuxChannel)
assert c.channel_id==7 and p.channel_id==7
assert c.stats=={'bytes_sent':0,'bytes_received':0,'frames_sent':0,'frames_received':0}

# data
c.send(b'hello'); c.send(b'world')
assert p.recv()==b'hello'; assert p.recv()==b'world'
assert c.stats['frames_sent']==2 and c.stats['bytes_sent']==10
assert p.stats['frames_received']==2 and p.stats['bytes_received']==10

# half close
c.shutdown('send')
try: c.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
p.send(b'back'); assert c.recv()==b'back'

# full close propagation
c.close()
try: c.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
try: p.recv(timeout=2); assert False
except EOFError: pass
try: p.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
assert not c.connected() and not p.connected()

# id reuse
c2=m1.open_channel(channel_id=7); p2=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
assert c2.channel_id==7

# close mux
m1.close(); m2.close()
assert m1.close() is None  # idempotent

# validation
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
for bad in [None, 0, 65536, 1.5, True]:
    try: TubeMultiplexer(l, max_channels=bad); assert False
    except ValueError: pass
try: TubeMultiplexer(l, low_water_mark=10, high_water_mark=5); assert False
except ValueError: pass
m=TubeMultiplexer(r)
try: m.open_channel(channel_id=True); assert False
except TypeError: pass
try: m.open_channel(channel_id=0); assert False
except ValueError: pass
m.close(); l.close()
print("FINAL SMOKE TEST PASSED")
PY
echo "=== doctests ==="
python -m doctest pwnlib/tubes/buffer.py >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "buffer doctest OK"
timeout 30 python - <<'PY'
import doctest, pwnlib.tubes.tube as tube_mod
doctest.run_docstring_examples(tube_mod.tube.mux, tube_mod.__dict__, verbose=False)
print("mux doctest OK")

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer, MuxChannel
from pwnlib.tubes.buffer import Buffer
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
import time
context.log_level='error'

print("buffer")
b=Buffer(); b.set_watermarks(high=100, low=50)
assert b.high_water==100 and b.low_water==50 and not b.over_high_water and b.under_low_water
b.add(b'A'*100); assert b.over_high_water and not b.under_low_water
b.get(50); assert not b.over_high_water and b.under_low_water
try: b.set_watermarks(high=1, low=2); assert False
except ValueError: pass

print("mux method")
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=l.mux(max_channels=64, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
m2=r.mux(max_channels=64, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
print("isinstance", isinstance(m1, TubeMultiplexer), isinstance(m2, TubeMultiplexer))
print("marks", m1.high_water_mark, m1.low_water_mark)
assert isinstance(m1, TubeMultiplexer) and isinstance(m2, TubeMultiplexer)
assert m1.high_water_mark==1000 and m1.low_water_mark==100

print("open")
c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=7); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
print("c", c, "p", p)
print("channels", list(m1.channels), list(m2.channels))
assert m1.channels[7] is c and m2.channels[7] is p
print("types", isinstance(c, tube), isinstance(c, MuxChannel), c.channel_id, p.channel_id)
assert isinstance(c, tube) and isinstance(c, MuxChannel)
assert c.channel_id==7 and p.channel_id==7
print("stats", c.stats)
assert c.stats=={'bytes_sent':0,'bytes_received':0,'frames_sent':0,'frames_received':0}

print("data")
c.send(b'hello'); c.send(b'world')
d=p.recv()
print("recv", d)
assert d==b'helloworld'
print("stats2", c.stats, p.stats)
assert c.stats['frames_sent']==2 and c.stats['bytes_sent']==10
assert p.stats['frames_received']==2 and p.stats['bytes_received']==10

print("half close")
c.shutdown('send')
try: c.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
p.send(b'back'); print("back recv", c.recv()); assert c.recv()==b'back'

print("full close")
c.close()
try: c.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
try: p.recv(timeout=2); assert False
except EOFError: pass
try: p.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
assert not c.connected() and not p.connected()

print("id reuse")
c2=m1.open_channel(channel_id=7); p2=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
assert c2.channel_id==7

m1.close(); m2.close()
assert m1.close() is None
print("ALL OK")
