b.get(50); assert not b.over_high_water and b.under_low_water
try: b.set_watermarks(high=1, low=2); assert False
except ValueError: pass
print("1 buffer ok")

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=l.mux(max_channels=64, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
m2=r.mux(max_channels=64, high_water_mark=1000, low_water_mark=100)
assert isinstance(m1, TubeMultiplexer) and isinstance(m2, TubeMultiplexer)
assert m1.high_water_mark==1000 and m1.low_water_mark==100
print("2 mux ok")

c=m1.open_channel(channel_id=7); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
assert m1.channels[7] is c and m2.channels[7] is p
assert isinstance(c, tube) and isinstance(c, MuxChannel)
assert c.channel_id==7 and p.channel_id==7
assert c.stats=={'bytes_sent':0,'bytes_received':0,'frames_sent':0,'frames_received':0}
print("3 open ok")

c.send(b'hello'); c.send(b'world')
r1=p.recvn(5); print("r1",r1); assert r1==b'hello'
r2=p.recvn(5); print("r2",r2); assert r2==b'world'
print("stats", c.stats, p.stats)
assert c.stats['frames_sent']==2 and c.stats['bytes_sent']==10
assert p.stats['frames_received']==2 and p.stats['bytes_received']==10
print("4 data ok")

c.shutdown('send')
try: c.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
p.send(b'back'); print("back", c.recvn(4)); assert c.recvn(4)==b'back'
print("5 halfclose ok")

c.close()
try: c.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
try: p.recv(timeout=2); assert False
except EOFError: pass
try: p.send(b'x'); assert False
except EOFError: pass
assert not c.connected() and not p.connected()
print("6 fullclose ok")

c2=m1.open_channel(channel_id=7); p2=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
assert c2.channel_id==7
print("7 reuse ok")

m1.close(); m2.close()
assert m1.close() is None
print("8 mux close ok")

l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
for bad in [None, 0, 65536, 1.5, True]:
    try: TubeMultiplexer(l, max_channels=bad); assert False
    except ValueError: pass
try: TubeMultiplexer(l, low_water_mark=10, high_water_mark=5); assert False
except ValueError: pass
m=TubeMultiplexer(r)
try: m.open_channel(channel_id=True); assert False
except TypeError: pass
try: m.open_channel(channel_id=0); assert False
except ValueError: pass
m.close(); l.close()
print("FINAL SMOKE TEST PASSED")

from pwn import *
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
import time
context.log_level='error'
# Clean close propagation, no prior half-close
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost', l.lport); l.wait_for_connection()
m1=TubeMultiplexer(l); m2=TubeMultiplexer(r)
c=m1.open_channel(); p=m2.accept_channel(timeout=3)
c.close()
# wait for close to propagate
for _ in range(50):
    if p.connected() is False:
        break
    time.sleep(0.01)
print('p connected after close:', p.connected())
try:
    p.send(b'x')
    print('FAIL: send did not raise')
except EOFError:
    print('peer send EOFError OK')
m1.close(); m2.close()

@@ -186,3 +188,43 @@ class Buffer(object):
 
         with context.local(buffer_size=size):
             return context.buffer_size
+
+    def set_watermarks(self, high=None, low=None):
+        """
+        Sets the high and low water marks used for flow control.
+
+        Arguments:
+            high(int): High water mark, at which ``over_high_water`` becomes
+                True.  ``None`` disables it.
+            low(int): Low water mark, at which ``under_low_water`` becomes
+                True.  ``None`` disables it.
+
+        Raises:
+            ValueError: If ``low > high``.
+        """
+        if high is not None and low is not None and low > high:
+            raise ValueError("low_water mark cannot exceed high_water mark")
+
+        self.high_water = high
+        self.low_water = low
+
+    @property
+    def over_high_water(self):
+        """
+        True when the buffer size is at or above the high water mark.
+        False when the high water mark is unset.
+        """
+        if self.high_water is None:
+            return False
+        return self.size >= self.high_water
+
+    @property
+    def under_low_water(self):
+        """
+        True when the buffer size is at or below the low water mark.
+        False when the low water mark is unset.
+        """
+        if self.low_water is None:
+            return False
+        return self.size <= self.low_water
+

+    def mux(self, **kwargs):
+        """mux(**kwargs) -> TubeMultiplexer
+
+        Wraps this tube in a :class:`pwnlib.tubes.mux.TubeMultiplexer`,
+        allowing multiple independent logical channels to be multiplexed
+        over this single tube.
+
+        Arguments passed to this method are forwarded to the
+        :class:`TubeMultiplexer` constructor.
+
+        Examples:
+
+            >>> from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
+            >>> from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube as _tube
+            >>> t = _tube()
+            >>> isinstance(t.mux(), TubeMultiplexer)
+            True
+        """
+        from pwnlib.tubes.mux import TubeMultiplexer
+        return TubeMultiplexer(self, **kwargs)

Implement a multiplexer that supports multiple independent, bidirectional
logical channels over a single underlying tube:

- New pwnlib.tubes.mux module with TubeMultiplexer and MuxChannel
- open_channel/accept_channel with handshake, validation and timeouts
- Per-channel flow control with configurable high/low water marks
- Channel close and half-close (shutdown) semantics with EOF propagation
- Underlying tube death propagates EOF to all channels
- Buffer.set_watermarks plus high_water/low_water/over_high_water/
  under_low_water properties
- tube.mux() convenience wrapper" && git log --oneline -2
